			activate_mission = GEH_basking_in_the_brilliance_of_their_serenade_mission

			hidden_effect = {
				GEH_prefect_team_spy = { unit_leader_event = { id = GEH.flavor_event.19 days = 100 random_days = 30 } }
			}
		}
	}

	focus = {
		id = GEH_Prefect_Team_muster
		icon = GFX_goal_Prefect_Team_muster
		cost = 5
		available = {
			if = { #ADVISOR WORKING ON THE FOCUS (Show progress)

				}
				set_temp_variable = { temp_focus_days = 5 } #For tooltip purposes
				multiply_temp_variable = { temp_focus_days = 7 } #The constant uses focus cost which is in weeks, so convert to days
				custom_override_tooltip = {
					tooltip = {
						localization_key = GER_inner_circle_focus_in_progress_tt
						CHARACTER = GEH_hina

				}
			}

				custom_override_tooltip = {

						FOCUS = GEH_zero_congress_focus
					}
					always = no

	focus = {
		id = GEH_second_congress_focus
		icon = GFX_goal_Perfect_team_first_congress
		cost = 5
		available = {
			has_completed_focus = GEH_zero_congress_focus
			has_completed_focus = GEH_focus_111111
        }
		prerequisite = {
			focus = GEH_start_focus
		}
        ai_will_do = {
            base = 80
        }
		offset = {
			trigger = {
				OR = {
					is_debug = yes
					has_completed_focus = GEH_zero_congress_focus
				}
			}
			x = 11
		}
        select_effect = {
			hidden_effect = {
				country_event = { id = GEH_hidden.3 }
			}
        }
        relative_position_id = GEH_zero_congress_focus
		x = 3
		y = 0
		completion_reward = {
			if = {
				limit = { has_country_flag = GEH_administrative_personnel_shortage_flag }
				set_country_flag = GEH_additional_leadership_personnel_employment_flag
				effect_tooltip = { unlock_national_focus = GEH_additional_leadership_personnel_employment }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { has_country_flag = GEH_establishment_of_german_autonomous_government_flag_flag }
				set_country_flag = GEH_establishment_of_german_autonomous_government_flag
				effect_tooltip = { unlock_national_focus = GEH_establishment_of_german_autonomous_government }
			}
			if = { #히나독재
				limit = { has_country_flag = hina_assembly_12_flag }
				add_stability = 0.05
				set_country_flag = hina_assembly_h_flag
				effect_tooltip = { unlock_national_focus = GEH_hina_route_focus }
			}
			else_if = { #아코
				limit = { check_variable = { GEH_hina_a_var > 3 } }
				add_stability = -0.03
				set_country_flag = hina_assembly_a_flag
				effect_tooltip = { unlock_national_focus = GEH_ako_route_focus }
			}
			else_if = { #이오리
				limit = { check_variable = { GEH_hina_c_var > 3 } }
				add_stability = -0.05
				set_country_flag = hina_assembly_c_flag
				effect_tooltip = { unlock_national_focus = GEH_iori_route_focus }
			}
			else = { #노멀
				add_stability = 0.03
				set_country_flag = hina_assembly_normal_flag
				effect_tooltip = { unlock_national_focus = GEH_main_route_focus }
			}
			mark_focus_tree_layout_dirty = yes
			
			hidden_effect = {
				news_event = { id = GEH_news.3 }
			}

			add_research_slot = 1 #4개
		}
	}

			}

		}

    focus = {
		id = GEH_last_focus_effect_a_rout
		icon = GFX_goal_GEH_last_focus_effect_a_rout
		cost = 2

		relative_position_id = GEH_last_focus_3
		x = 0
		y = 2

		allow_branch = {
			OR = {
				is_debug = yes
				has_country_flag = hina_assembly_a_next_flag
			}
		}
		
		offset = {
			x = 20
			trigger = {
				is_debug = yes
			}
		}

		available = {
			hidden_trigger = {
				has_country_flag = hina_assembly_a_next_flag
			}
		}
        prerequisite = {
            focus = GEH_last_focus_3
        }
		ai_will_do = {
			base = 100
			modifier = {
				factor = 2
				has_war = no
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.5
				has_war = yes
			}
		}
		completion_reward = {
			add_popularity = {
				ideology = democratic
				popularity = 0.6
			}
	
			set_politics = {
				ruling_party = democratic #히나 민주주의 리더
				elections_allowed = no
			}        
	
			GEH_hina = {
				add_country_leader_role = {
					promote_leader = yes
					country_leader = {
						ideology = technocracy
						expire = "1980.1.1"
						desc = "GEH_hina_ako_route_base_leader_desc"
						traits = {
							b_tjsehqnwkd_week GEH_military_chairman
						}
					}
				}
			}
			
			set_rule = { CAN_DECLARE_WAR_ON_SAME_IDEOLOGY = yes }
			set_rule = { can_only_justify_war_on_threat_country = no }
			set_rule = { can_join_opposite_factions = yes }    

			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { GEH_ako_democratic_var > 2 }
					#GEH_ako_democratic_loc_pro_kivotos
				}
				country_event = { id = GEH.ako.gov_event.3 }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { GEH_ako_democratic_var < -2 }
					#GEH_ako_democratic_loc_pro_german
				}
				country_event = { id = GEH.ako.gov_event.5 }
			}
			else = {
				country_event = { id = GEH.ako.gov_event.4 }
			}
		}
	}

    focus = {
		id = GEH_three_congress_focus_no_hina
		icon = GFX_goal_GEH_sad_hina
		cost = 5

		relative_position_id = GEH_three_congress_focus
		x = 3
		y = 0
		text_icon = GER_inner_circle_focus_style

		mutually_exclusive = {
			focus = GEH_three_congress_focus
		}

		allow_branch = {
			OR = {
				is_debug = yes
				has_completed_focus = GEH_start_focus
			}
		}
		
		offset = {
			x = 2
			y = 13
			trigger = {
				is_debug = yes
			}
		}

		available = {
			if = { #ADVISOR WORKING ON THE FOCUS (Show progress)
				limit = {
					has_country_flag = GEH_three_congress_focus_no_hina_in_progress_flag
				}
				set_temp_variable = { temp_focus_days = 5 } #For tooltip purposes
				multiply_temp_variable = { temp_focus_days = 7 } #The constant uses focus cost which is in weeks, so convert to days
				custom_override_tooltip = {
					tooltip = {
						localization_key = GER_inner_circle_focus_in_progress_tt
						CHARACTER = GEH_dkzh
						FLAG_DAYS = [?GEH_three_congress_focus_no_hina_in_progress_flag:days]
					}
					always = no 
				}
			}
			else = { #NOT BEEN WORKED ON (Just Explain)
				custom_override_tooltip = {
					tooltip = {
						localization_key = GEH_three_congress_focus_no_hina_can_only_be_completed_by_focus_tt
						CHARACTER = GEH_dkzh
						FOCUS = GEH_zero_congress_focus
					}
					always = no
				}
			}
		}
        
		ai_will_do = {
			base = 100
			modifier = {
				factor = 2
				has_war = no
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.5
				has_war = yes
			}
		}

		completion_reward = {
			if = { # 히나 독재
				limit = { has_country_flag = hina_assembly_h_flag }
				custom_effect_tooltip = GEH_three_congress_focus_no_hina_htt
				set_country_flag = hina_assembly_h_serenade_flag
			}
			else_if = { # 아코
				limit = { has_country_flag = hina_assembly_a_flag }
				custom_effect_tooltip = GEH_three_congress_focus_no_hina_att
				set_country_flag = hina_assembly_a_serenade_flag
			}
			else_if = { # 이오리
				limit = { has_country_flag = hina_assembly_c_flag }
				custom_effect_tooltip = GEH_three_congress_focus_no_hina_ctt
				set_country_flag = hina_assembly_c_serenade_flag
			}
			else_if = { # 기본
				limit = { has_country_flag = hina_assembly_normal_flag }
				custom_effect_tooltip = GEH_three_congress_focus_no_hina_ntt
				set_country_flag = hina_assembly_normal_serenade_flag

				set_politics = {
					ruling_party = neutrality
					elections_allowed = no
				}

				set_portraits = {
					character = GEH_dkzh
					civilian = { large ="gfx/leaders/GEH/GEH_ako_amau_leader.dds" }
				}

				GEH_dkzh = {
					promote_leader = yes
				}
				
				set_country_leader_description = {
					desc = GEH_ako_amau_nomal_route_bad_ending_leader_desc
				}
			}
			else = {
				custom_effect_tooltip = GEH_three_congress_focus_no_hina_ftt
				if = { 
					limit = {
						is_subject = no
						any_other_country = {
							OR = {
								has_country_flag = GEH_unified_gehenna_flag
								original_tag = GEM
							}
						}
					}
					if = {
						limit = {
							any_other_country = {
								original_tag = GEM
							}
						}
						random_country = {
							limit = { original_tag = GEM }
							annex_country = {
								target = ROOT
								transfer_troops = yes
							}
						}
					}
					else = {
						random_country = {
							limit = { has_country_flag = GEH_unified_gehenna_flag }
							annex_country = {
								target = ROOT
								transfer_troops = yes
							}
						}
					}
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = {
						is_subject = no
						has_capitulated = no
					}
					every_enemy_country = {
						set_country_flag = GEH_create_dynamic_country_enemy_flag
					}
					create_dynamic_country = {
						original_tag = GEH
						set_cosmetic_tag = gehenna_unification_cosmetic
						hidden_effect = {
							add_popularity = {
								ideology = neutrality
								popularity = 1
							}
							set_politics = {
								ruling_party = neutrality
							}
							add_ideas = BA_student_conscription_idea
							kill_ideology_leader = neutrality
							kill_ideology_leader = democratic
							kill_ideology_leader = fascism
							kill_ideology_leader = communism
						}
						annex_country = {
							target = ROOT
							transfer_troops = yes
						}
						every_country = {
							limit = { has_country_flag = GEH_create_dynamic_country_enemy_flag }
							declare_war_on = {
								target = PREV
								type = annex_everything
							}
							clr_country_flag = GEH_create_dynamic_country_enemy_flag
						}
					}
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = {
						is_subject = yes
					}
					OVERLORD = {
						annex_country = {
							target = ROOT
							transfer_troops = yes
						}
					}
				}
			}
			mark_focus_tree_layout_dirty = yes
		}
	}

		y = 1

				is_debug = yes
				has_country_flag = hina_assembly_c_serenade_flag
			}
		}
		
		offset = {
			x = 25
			trigger = {
				is_debug = yes
			}
		}

		available = {
			hidden_trigger = {
				has_country_flag = hina_assembly_c_serenade_flag
			}
		}
        prerequisite = {
            focus = GEH_three_congress_focus_no_hina
        }
		ai_will_do = {
			base = 100
			modifier = {
				factor = 2
				has_war = no
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.5
				has_war = yes
			}
		}
		completion_reward = {
			if = {
				limit = { 
					has_country_leader = {
						ruling_only = yes # default = yes
						character = GEH_prefect_team_emergency_committee
					}
				}
				GEH_prefect_team_emergency_committee = {
					retire = yes
				}
			}
			add_popularity = {
				ideology = fascism
				popularity = 0.4
			}

			set_politics = {
				ruling_party = fascism #이오리 리더
				elections_allowed = no
			}            
			
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = {
						has_country_leader = {
							character = GEH_dldhfl
							ruling_only = no
						}
					}
				}
				add_country_leader_role = {
					character = GEH_dldhfl
					promote_leader = yes
					country_leader = {
						ideology = fascism_ideology
						expire = "1980.1.1"
						traits = {
							dictator_1
							GEH_blame_director
						}
					}
				}
			}
			else = {
				promote_character = GEH_dldhfl
			}
			set_party_name = {
				ideology = neutrality
				long_name = GEH_anti_iori_party_long
				name = GEH_anti_iori_party
			}

			GEH_dkzh = {
				add_country_leader_role = {
					promote_leader = yes
					country_leader = {
						ideology = oligarchism
						expire = "1980.1.1"
						traits = {
						}
					}
				}
			}

			add_ideas = GEH_kriegsrecht
		}
	}

		}

			focus = GEH_prefect_team_luftwaffe_anti_air
		}

				name = secret_bonus

				category = nuclear
				category = rocketry
			}

				specialization = specialization_nuclear
				value = 0.5
			}
		}
	}
